Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any combinatorial module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 23. The projective resolution has length at most 42 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on combinatorial structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 23. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 42. QED.

Theorem 214.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For invariance principle pairs (A,B) in combinatorial theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 114 and the invariant I(A,B) = 649 mod 4.

Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
